# Grundstück der Gemarkung Gonzenheim, je Flur 8, Flurstück 122/11, Gebäude- und Freifläche, Auf der Steinkaut 10 mit 1.145 m² und Flurstück 123/1, Gebäude- und Freifläche, Auf der Steinkaut 10-12 mit 5.216 m², eingetragen im Grundbuch des Amtsgerichts Bad Homburg v.d. Höhe von Gonzenheim Blatt 2967 unter lfd. Nr. 3 des Bestandsverzeichnisses
asset_uuid 1cca8119 · 30 files · read 02 Sep 2026

## What this asset is

The asset is the Amtsgericht Bad Homburg v.d.H., a court building at Auf der Steinkaut 10-12, 61348 Bad Homburg, with a documented net floor area of 6047 m². Its identification and description rest on the land-register details for the Gonzenheim parcels and the energy certificate, which records the building’s main use as a court building.

## Condition and repair

- The report über Baugrund und Gründung beim Neubauvorhaben Amtsgericht Bad Homburg v.d.H. "Auf der Steinkaut", Bad Homburg v.d.H. concerns the soil conditions and foundations of the new courthouse project, including die ausgeführten Untersuchungsbohrungen für das Bauvorhaben: - Neubau Amtsgericht Bad Homburg v.d.H. -, and one water sample from Bohrung 3 at 3.00 m Tiefe for Bad Homburg v.d.H., Gericht. (1963-08-08 Bodengutachten Hess.Landesamt fÅr Bodenforschung 08.08.1963.pdf, pages 1, 3 and 4, 1963-08-08.) (c2e80b4c, 26a003c3, 79c57832)
- The groundwater's unstressed level is NN + 155 bis 157 m, it was encountered by drilling at NN + 153 m bis + 154 m and, depending on slope position, at depths between 2,50 m (Nr.3) and 2,70 m (Nr.5); according to the Schichtenbild, the groundwater conditions are fairly uniform and normal, but the groundwater is schwach kohlensäureaggressiv. (1963-08-08 Bodengutachten Hess.Landesamt fÅr Bodenforschung 08.08.1963.pdf, pages 1 and 3, 1963-08-08.) (010d6036, 7864c4d9, b4403a28)
- A chemische Untersuchung des Grundwassers zur Betonaggressivität was carried out, and the attached Untersuchungsbefund is specifically referenced. (1963-08-08 Bodengutachten Hess.Landesamt fÅr Bodenforschung 08.08.1963.pdf, page 3, 1963-08-08.) (f2badc83)
- All foundations will stand in the zone of the Basalttuffes, with Gründungssohle NN + 155 m and Heizungskeller NN + 153,5 m; either Streifenfundamente or Einzelfundamente may be chosen. (1963-08-08 Bodengutachten Hess.Landesamt fÅr Bodenforschung 08.08.1963.pdf, page 2, 1963-08-08.) (79ceba5c, 3587c96c)
- The recommended design is a steifen Kellerkasten with Stahlbetonkellerwände und Kellerdecke and massive Beton-Decken in the Geschossen, allowing the same Sohlpressung for all foundations without Setzungsspannungen causing damage. (1963-08-08 Bodengutachten Hess.Landesamt fÅr Bodenforschung 08.08.1963.pdf, page 2, 1963-08-08.) (3259fa27, 904514b2)
- The Sohlpressungen may be up to 2,5 kg/cm², plus 25 % for Kantenpressungen; the connecting section should have Setzfugen on both sides, and one wing is somewhat long at 49 m if it cannot be subdivided. (1963-08-08 Bodengutachten Hess.Landesamt fÅr Bodenforschung 08.08.1963.pdf, page 2, 1963-08-08.) (b8bf413e, 927835f3)
- The groundwater conditions require particular attention; with a Vorfluterniveau of NN + 152,5 m, the Normalkeller should be capable of being kept dry by a surrounding and cross-running Dräne, whereas reliable Niederhaltung des Grundwassers below the Heizungskeller level of NN + 153,5 m is more difficult and a druckwasserhaltende Wanne is recommended there. (1963-08-08 Bodengutachten Hess.Landesamt fÅr Bodenforschung 08.08.1963.pdf, page 2, 1963-08-08.) (6679b4ff, 5116d40a, 9b0a1075)
- For rooms outside such a Wanne, the report recommends Sperrbeton and otherwise a sehr gefügedichter Beton; the Dräne must have Kontroll- und Reinigungsschächte at every bend and be made of aggresionsbeständigem Material, preferably gelochte und derauf glasierte Tonrohre, with the Filterkiesschüttung raised from the drain pipes to just below OK Gelände. (1963-08-08 Bodengutachten Hess.Landesamt fÅr Bodenforschung 08.08.1963.pdf, page 2, 1963-08-08.) (88c8c88e, 56b87ed0, 8e05d7e6)

- The recorded electricity consumption for 01.01.2019 bis 31.12.2019 is 121770 kWh, and the building's final energy consumption for electricity is 21 kWh/(m²·a); this figure includes hot water, ventilation, installed lighting, cooling and other consumption, while the additional-heating box is not checked. [16–18] (bd3a59ce, f39515aa, a7f84245)
- Energy demand is shown through annual primary energy demand and final energy demand for heating, hot water, built-in lighting, ventilation and cooling; these figures are calculated rather than based on measured consumption, from construction documents or building-specific data using standardized boundary conditions, including climate, usage, indoor temperature and internal heat gains, and therefore cannot be used to determine actual energy consumption. [21–24] (44c3b22a, d2efe7e8, 3d194cee, dc149d59)
- Primary energy demand represents the building’s energy efficiency and includes both final energy and the upstream energy chain, including exploration, extraction, distribution and conversion, for the relevant energy sources; the scale value for primary energy demand is rounded to the nearest ten and is three times the comparison value for the modernised existing building, which equals 140% of the EnEV new-building requirement value. [25–27] (1cdfae7f, 5f5337e7, cb620229)
- The EnEV imposes requirements on the energy quality of heat-transmitting envelope surfaces, including Außenwände, Decken and Fenster, and, for new buildings, on summer heat protection against overheating. [28] (53a4994a)
- Final energy demand is the technically calculated annual energy quantity required for heating, hot water, built-in lighting, ventilation and cooling; it is calculated using standard climate and standard usage conditions and indicates the standard efficiency of the building and its technical systems, with a lower value indicating lower demand and higher energy efficiency. [29–32] (60c626e1, dd10c1ac, d70114b0, 02e5170d)
- Final consumption of heat and electricity is determined from heating-cost statements or energy-supplier bills, based on the building’s individual user units rather than the billing period; the resulting values are specific consumption values per square metre of net floor area under the EnEV, and recorded heating consumption is adjusted using local weather data and climate factors to a Germany-wide average. [33–36] (efa65a08, 14710654, 3f42bf5d, 46b6f35f)
- Final energy consumption figures provide an indication of the building’s energy quality, and a lower value indicates lower consumption; however, the figures cannot be used to predict expected future consumption, and actual consumption may differ because of weather, changing user behaviour or changing uses. [37–40] (b84e3a27, 25f22201, cc0508f9, 3e954b6d)
- For longer periods of vacancy, a standard surcharge is calculated and included in the recorded consumption; whether and to what extent it was included is shown in the table "Verbrauchserfassung". Comparison values are derived by assessing similar buildings, and consumption below the comparison value indicates good energy quality relative to the existing stock of that building type; the endpoint values of both final-energy-consumption columns are rounded to the nearest ten and equal twice the respective comparison value. (0c4ac374, f765e767, 6d58373c, a993f437, 53dc89b7)

- The defect legend states: 4 = Zarge nicht fachgerecht eingebaut / defekt / lose, meaning that the door frame was improperly installed, defective or loose; 5 = Türgummi fehlt / defekt / zu kurz, meaning that the door seal was missing, defective or too short; 6 = Obertürschließer /Bodentürschließer/ Federband defekt, meaning that the overhead door closer, floor door closer or spring hinge was defective; 7 = Absenkbare Bodendichtung fehlt/ defekt, meaning that the drop-down floor seal was missing or defective; and 8 = Auflaufschwelle nicht dicht am Boden, meaning that the threshold did not seal tightly against the floor. (5084a346, 00fcf2d8, 3a122fd2, 65a0329b, 300835a3)
- The defect legend further states: 9 = Schließfolge defekt, meaning that the closing sequence was defective; 10 = Türblatt verzogen, meaning that the door leaf was warped; 11 = Aufschaumdichtung fehlt, meaning that the expanding foam seal was missing; 12 = Türgriff defekt / fehlt, meaning that the door handle was defective or missing; and 13 = Panikverriegelung defekt/Panikverriegelung fehlt komplett, meaning that the panic locking mechanism was defective or completely missing. (3b4a80f6, 7d0aeef4, d6faf70e, bac5dd9f, ba54f5bc)
- The defect legend further states: 14 = Blindzylinder fehlt, bzw. Schließzylinder, indicating that the blank cylinder or locking cylinder was missing; 15 = Tür schließt nicht, meaning that the door did not close; 16 = Schlosskasten defekt, meaning that the lock case was defective; and 17 = RM/ Raucherkennungsteil laut Hersteller abgelaufen, meaning that the RM smoke-detection component had expired according to the manufacturer. (e00d9901, 2ced41d8, 4e733e35, 23260fa3)
- The 2024-09-30 condition report records the following defects for L2-03: 18 = Deckenmelder fehlen (über 1m), meaning ceiling detectors are missing where the ceiling height is over 1 metre; 19 = FSA ohne Funktion (Magnet hält nicht) Plattenmagnet defekt, meaning the hold-open system does not function because the magnet does not hold or the plate magnet is defective; 20 = FSA löst nicht aus, meaning the hold-open system does not release or trigger; 21 = Obertürschließer Zweiflüglig fehlen - Gleitschiene, meaning the overhead door closer for a double-leaf door, including the slide rail, is missing; 22 = Obertürschließer fehlt Einflüglig, meaning the overhead door closer for a single-leaf door is missing; 23 = Löcher in Tür, meaning there are holes in the door; 24 = Standflügelverriegelung defekt, meaning the inactive door leaf locking mechanism is defective; 25 = Tür war verkeilt, meaning the door was wedged open; 26 = Besondere Mängel an Brandschutztüren, meaning there are special defects in fire doors; 27 = Obertürschließer ist ausgehängt, meaning the overhead door closer has been unhooked; 28 = Raucherkennungsteil RM verschmutzt / defekt, meaning the RM smoke-detection component is dirty or defective; and 29 = Einbau Feststellanlage empfohlen, meaning installation of a hold-open system is recommended. - The legally required proof of competence is a condition for performing inspection, maintenance and repair; each inspector must keep it updated and submit it to FMC planen + bauen AG before carrying out the work, and where deviations are identified, the plans and inspection reports must be updated during the work by recording changes in the plan, using an accompanying sheet if necessary, with those changes submitted to FMC planen + bauen AG with the Prüfbericht. - The applied coating system follows the contours of the existing substrate, so it will not materially improve levelness or slope, and uncontrolled puddles may form unless the substrate provides deliberate surface drainage; prices for coloured coatings and paints assume standard colours from the product supplier’s colour chart, while special colours may require a surcharge. (Facts 39–40) (a7a5b377, ed9464de)
- The 2025-07-04 Vergabe, Sanierung TG - Angebot Fa. Chemicon.pdf states that Kunstharzbeschichtungen generally age and may undergo Farbtonveränderung (Vergilbung) depending on the selected colour and UV exposure; Weichmacherunterwanderungen cannot be excluded for certain resins, but these changes do not impair the coating system’s function and are not a Gewährleistungsmangel. (89951641)
- The 2025-07-04 Vergabe, Sanierung TG - Angebot Fa. Chemicon.pdf states that the work may be carried out only under the relevant temperature and humidity conditions, and that Witterungsschutzmaßnahmen are not included in the prices. (0e69b6e8)
- The 2025-07-04 Vergabe, Sanierung TG - Angebot Fa. Chemicon.pdf states that Wartungsfugen, as defined among others by DIN 52460 and IVD Merkblatt Nr. 15, must be inspected regularly and renewed where necessary to avoid consequential damage; Fugensanierungen and all listed elastic-sealant joints are excluded from Gewährleistung because of construction-related heavy wear. (64205d79)
- The 2025-07-04 Vergabe, Sanierung TG - Angebot Fa. Chemicon.pdf states that damage arising from the substrate or from unknown construction-related causes is excluded from Gewährleistung. (1a8fefd3)
- The 2025-07-04 Vergabe, Sanierung TG - Angebot Fa. Chemicon.pdf states that only the allgemeinen Lieferbedingungen für Erzeugnisse und Leistungen der Elektroindustrie (ZVEI) apply; Elektrokomponenten außerhalb des Betons have a Gewährleistung of 24 Monaten, and failed Referenzelektroden embedded in concrete are to be replaced after the Gewährleistung only where meaningful interpretation of data within a Schutzone is no longer possible. (0a434f59)
- The 2025-07-04 Vergabe, Sanierung TG - Angebot Fa. Chemicon.pdf states that, following the bauaufsichtliche Einführung der TR-Instandhaltung, the parties involved must document the Verwendbarkeit of Instandsetzungsprodukte and Oberflächenschutzsysteme using Bauwerks- und Bauteilspezifischen Leistungsmerkmalen; because the required Verwendbarkeitsnachweise, including Dokumentationen nach Art. 30 Bau-PVO, are unavailable or incomplete in the market, Chemicon will provide technical documentation based on the respective manufacturer’s evidence if commissioned, which must be validated by the Sachkundigen Planer for suitability under the Instandsetzungsplan and approved in writing by the client before execution. (db9612a8)

- The 20260122_PATRIZIA - Project Covenant_Kostentabelle_Bad Homburg_v01 final.xlsx, sheet "Maßnahmen", dated 2026-01-20, records that the concrete walls of the underground-garage entrance have damage and exposed reinforcement; Sanierung der Betonwände einschl. (380eda46)
- The roof windows with RWA-Funktion at the Treppenhausköpfe are worn and are to be renewed mittelfristig; the estimated net cost is 11500 EUR and liability is marked L. (ed9d9bb0)
- Fall protection or fall-protective glazing is missing at the windows, including possibly in several rooms; Nachrüsten von Geländern (Absturzsicherung) an Fenstern is required sofort, at an estimated net cost of 3450 EUR with liability marked L, and the entry refers to DIN 18008-4. (d1f5033c)
- The windows and external doors require repairs due to use and age, including increased repair work to Holz-Aluminiumfenster with partial wear and brittle sill infills; the work is planned mittelfristig at an estimated net cost of 45000 EUR with liability marked L. (dc4ca555)
- The stair railing does not meet the requirements of DIN 18065 Durchsturzsicherung because of bar spacing <12cm; Ertüchtigung Treppengeländer zur Einhaltung der Abstände zu Treppenläufen aus z.B. Plexiglas is required sofort, at an estimated net cost of 7200 EUR with liability marked L. (1a60970d)
- The underground garage has substantial concrete damage and reinforcement corrosion; Betonsanierung der Tiefgarage komplett einschl. Schutzanstrich is planned mittelfristig and was reportedly already commissioned, at an estimated net cost of 1000000 EUR with liability marked L. (ddcc8cf5)
- No current Flucht- und Rettungspläne are available, or they are not up to date; Anbringen und umpositionieren von Fluchtwegpiktogrammen is required sofort, at an estimated net cost of 11500 EUR with liability marked T. (8e7a3812)
- The Brandmeldezentrale will reach the end of its useful life during the assessment period or is, or will be, discontinued by the manufacturer; Erneuerung der Brandmeldezentrale is planned langfristig at an estimated net cost of 27500 EUR with liability marked T. (29364513)
- Sample inspection found Rohr- und/oder Kabeldurchdringungen bzw. MLAR/M-LüAR sofort, with costs potentially higher or lower depending on the findings, at an estimated net cost of 11500 EUR with liability marked T. (4ec63c68)
- Automatic fire detectors are to be renewed after no more than 8 years under DIN 14675-1; Erneuerung von automatische Brandmeldern is listed both kurzfristig and langfristig, in each case at an estimated net cost of 15500 EUR with liability marked T. (b510b03f, a152a02b)

- Terrorversicherung / Terrorism insurance: Dieser Vertrag bietet keine Terrordeckung. Lokale Policen im Rahmen des Vertrages bieten Deckung über die lokalen Terror-Pools: TRIP, Consorcio, GAREAT and NHT. - The zur Funktionsfähigkeit und Benutzbarkeit der Mietsache dienenden Teile der Außenanlagen include, for example, Einfriedungen, Schrankenanlagen, Straßen, Stellplätze and Fußwege; where their functionality and usability depend on electrical supply, all stromversorgenden Teile, including Antrieb, Elektroleitung and Steuerung, are Bestandteile des jeweiligen Bauteils. (d1e7cab8, c027cf93)
- Where Anlagen, die zu Dach und Fach gehören, are controlled through a Bussystem in connection with the Technische Gebäudeausrüstung, the tenant must carry out the Instandsetzung und Instandhaltung of the Bussystems. (a6a3a4ab)
- The jeweilige Vermieter must continuously ensure an outer appearance appropriate to the use of the relevant building, while the tenant is otherwise responsible at its own cost for the Instandhaltung und Instandsetzung of the Mietsache, including Wartung, Inspektion and necessary Ersetzung/Erneuerung. (4affec98, d25c9a86)
- The jeweilige Vermieter must perform its work under § 6.1 within a reasonable period; for defects existing at the start of the lease, § 3.2 applies, and if the landlord fails to act on time after a warning and additional deadline, the tenant may have the necessary work performed at the landlord’s cost. (57f38c77)
- The tenant must carry out its § 6.2 work when the Mietverhältnis ends, while during the Mietzeit it decides whether to carry it out nach billigem Ermessen; in an imminent-danger situation, each party must arrange measures to eliminate the danger. (d90f4158, cad7b135)
- The Durchführung der Schönheitsreparaturen is Sache des Mieters, but the tenant has no obligation to perform or pay for them, including related Betriebs- und Nebenkosten, and the jeweilige Vermieter is likewise not obliged to perform or pay for them; the same allocation applies to Begrünung und Bepflanzung of the Mietsache in the Innen- or Außenbereich. (afc4f58e, 3d9fdc54)
- Before substantial maintenance or repair work for which it is responsible begins, the jeweilige Vermieter must coordinate the method of execution with the tenant, including any higher Qualitätsstandard or abweichende Ausführung such as Farbgestaltung; the document's sentence continues beyond the page, and requested Qualitätsstandards or an abweichende Ausführung must be taken into account if the tenant agrees to reimburse the resulting Mehrkosten. (d676f122, d88cf221)
- When the Mietverhältnis ends, the tenant must vacate and return the Mietsache besenrein, including clearing it of Untermietern, Unterpächtern and Untermutzern, subject to its maintenance and repair obligation under § 6.2; apartments rented to third parties under § 565 BGB are excluded. (5fce7c85)
- At the end of the Mietverhältnis, Betriebsvorrichtungen i.S.d. § 68 Abs. 2 BewG that are wesentlicher Bestandteil des Grundbesitzes, are not Teile von Dach und Fach gemäß § 6, and existed when the contract was concluded remain with the Vermieter against agreed compensation corresponding to their Restwert or are removed by the tenant, at the tenant’s choice; Investitionen and werterhöhende Maßnahmen on those Betriebsvorrichtungen are not compensated. (1aa38484)
- The jeweilige Vermieter may carry out bauliche Veränderungen necessary to avert imminent danger or remedy damage without the tenant’s consent, but must consider the tenant’s interests and avoid disruption of its Geschäftsbetrieb where possible; other bauliche Veränderungen by the landlord require the tenant’s vorherige schriftliche Zustimmung. (e20bd193, 11c062cf)
- The tenant may alter the Mietsache and install Einrichtungen without the jeweilige Vermieter’s consent unless the work affects Dach und Fach within § 6; changes to room layouts through Leichtbauwände and tenant-side Verkabelung likewise require no consent, while other changes require the landlord’s consent. (d00193cb, cd7346aa)
- The Zustimmung may be refused only for an important reason, which exists only where the bauliche Veränderung more than insignificantly impairs the value of the building or the Nutzungsmöglichkeit of the Mietsache after the Mietverhältnis ends, unless the tenant undertakes to carry out the Rückbau at its own cost when the Mietverhältnis ends. (76a5f963, e8c8331d)
- Before carrying out changes requiring consent, the tenant must provide the jeweilige Vermieter with suitable plans; consent is deemed granted if the landlord does not object, stating reasons, within vier Wochen after receiving the tenant’s written notice. (359de685, c5d29a4f)
- The tenant must obtain any required baurechtliche Genehmigungen at its own cost, with the jeweilige Vermieter providing support where necessary; on request, the landlord must provide existing documents needed for the changes, including Baugenehmigungen, statische Nachweise, Wärmeschutzberechnungen and Pläne. (df160905, 15cf8a88)
- The tenant is liable for all Schäden connected with carrying out changes and must freistellen the jeweilige Vermieter from related third-party claims; it must also indemnify the landlord against increased Instandhaltungs-, Instandsetzungs- or Versicherungskosten resulting from its bauliche Veränderungen that would otherwise be borne by the landlord. (a7c0e340, aca0a2f3)
- Where the tenant is entitled under § 9 to alter the Mietsache, it is not required but is entitled to restore the ursprünglichen Zustand when the Mietverhältnis ends. (b41f80b4)
- Structural changes still carried out by the seller, acting as tenant, under § 12.1 of the purchase contracts and Anlagen KV 12.1 (1) bis (39) zur Bezugsurkunde are tolerated by the tenant; the leased property includes the object together with those changes, the tenant has no obligation to restore them when the tenancy ends, and § 9.3 Absätze 2 und 3 apply correspondingly. (96490e68, f79adf98, 466980e6, d67ed7a1)
- The tenant is considering, but is not obliged to carry out, the structural changes described in Anlagen MV 9.6 (1) bis (39) zur Bezugsurkunde; the respective landlord has already expressly addressed those changes, although the sentence continues beyond the cited page, and the leased property includes them with no tenant restoration obligation at the end of the tenancy; § 9.3 Absätze 2 bis 5 apply correspondingly. (11300def, 6737844d, 2afb9a73)
- Each landlord, or the landlord’s appointed or authorised representatives, may inspect the leased property during the tenant’s reasonable business hours after prior notification; in cases of imminent danger, the tenant must allow the relevant landlord access at any time, and on entering the leased property the landlord must give particular consideration to the tenant’s interests, including work processes, hazardous areas, confidentiality requirements, security matters and judicial requirements. (32b6aa1d, f42204c3, 753519c5)
- The tenant may sublet or sublease the leased property or parts of it, or assign apartments located in it to state employees under the applicable Hessian civil-servant housing rules, without the landlord’s consent; each landlord acknowledges that parts have already been sublet, subleased or assigned as official housing, and the tenant must notify the relevant landlord of any further such arrangements. (dc8215fb, 19e7dae7, 4b540b80)
- The relevant landlord is responsible for premises safety to the extent that, under § 6.1, the landlord must maintain and repair Dach und Fach; otherwise, including snow and ice removal and gritting, responsibility lies with the tenant. (676f0836)
- Each landlord must take out Haus- und Grundbesitzer- sowie Gewässerschadenshaftpflichtversicherung and Allgefahren-Sachversicherung, including cover for examples such as Brand, Explosion, Elementarschäden and böswillige Beschädigungen, but the stated insurance protection is exclusively against Schäden durch Terrorismus; the landlord must select insurers and conclude insurance contracts in agreement with the tenant. (d72f6e23, 370efb98, 9fb0d3b3)
- The Land Hessen approves and applies for registration of a beschränkte persönliche Dienstbarkeit over the Grundbesitz identified in § 1.1, in favour of the Land Hessen as Begünstigter, allowing the Land Hessen to use the Grundstück nebst aufstehenden Gebäuden for its purposes, including—among other uses—commercial office, business, practice, training, residential, technical, ancillary and Kfz-Stellplätze uses and any further use permitted under public law. (45faf05d, 2ad0bdba)
- The Dienstbarkeit must be registered in the Grundbuch at a rangbereite Stelle but ahead of all rights in Abt. III des Grundbuchs. (417ea0ce)
- The beschränkte persönliche Dienstbarkeit ends when any listed auflösende Bedingung occurs, including termination of the tenancy between the relevant landlord and tenant following ordinary Kündigung under § 2.3 or § 2.4, extraordinary Kündigung by the relevant landlord for reasons attributable to the tenant, expiry of time, or mutual termination; these conditions must be entered in the Grundbuch as part of the content of the Dienstbarkeit. (24388518, 86e91ce7, 96899075, 34008b8d, 648b107c)
- Exercise of the Dienstbarkeit may be delegated to third parties, but the relevant landlord and the Begünstigter agree that this is permitted only to the extent specified by the Begünstigter; the cited sentence continues beyond the page, and the relevant party is entitled to sublet under the Mietvertrag between that party and the respective landlord. (104ab185, 39fe8452, 8a269bc9)
- Use under the Dienstbarkeit is free during the term of the Mietvertrag; after the Mietvertrag has ended, the tenant must pay a Nutzungsentgelt equivalent to the rent under that Mietvertrag and bear the Betriebs- und Nebenkosten under § 5.2. (647ea76a)
- The Begünstigte bears the costs of creating, registering and deleting the Dienstbarkeit; its stated value is EUR 10.000,--, as recorded in the cited undated document, and the parties must transfer all rights and obligations under the arrangement, including that obligation, in full to any Rechtsnachfolger. (c20d9ec7, d00687c4, cd1b9047)
- The contract contains all agreements between the contracting parties concerning the Mietverhältnis and there are no oral side agreements; amendments and additions, and all declarations for which the contract prescribes Schriftform, must be in writing, and the Schriftform requirement may be waived only in writing. (f779c9cb, 708852b5, 63b9161c)
- The Mietvertragsparteien acknowledge the special Schriftformerfordernisse of §§ 550, 578 BGB and must, whenever requested by a party, take all necessary actions and make all declarations required to satisfy them, undertaking not to terminate the Mietvertrag early by relying on non-compliance with Schriftform; this applies to the Ursprungs-/Hauptvertrag and all Nachtrags-, Änderungs- und Ergänzungsverträge. (11c6b731)
- At a party’s request, the Mietvertragsparteien must prepare and sign, in zweifacher Ausfertigung, a separate Mietvertragsurkunde based on this Mietvertrag for each individual Mietsache and its associated Anlagen; the cited sentence continues beyond the page, and if such a separate document is created, the parties must execute a Nachtrag stating that only the two copies of the new document govern the relevant Mietvertragsbeziehungen and Mietsache. (0c1afa33, ddb4701a)
- The tenant may install Schilder und Hinweistafeln on or at the Mietsache at its reasonable discretion under § 315 BGB, using the tenant’s Behörden-Design; the tenant must obtain öffentlich-rechtliche Genehmigungen and comply with behördliche Auflagen at its own expense. (b31865f8, 01107bf8)
- On termination of the lease, the tenant must remove signs and notice boards that were not already present at the start of the lease and repair, at its own cost, damage caused by their installation, operation or removal; the tenant may display a reasonably sized sign referring to its new premises at a visible location of its choice for at least three months after termination and must then remove it at its own expense. (Facts 1–2) (2dc85386, 67410bac)
- Each landlord must require personnel working at or administering the leased property to keep confidential the internal affairs of the tenant and other users; the tenant states that documents, records, files, card indexes and data media in the official rooms are subject to general and special data-protection rules, official secrecy and other confidentiality obligations, including tax secrecy. (Facts 3–4) (927ebc77, 42c3c24c)
- Personnel must not inspect those materials or open cabinets, drawers or similar items without authorisation and must keep confidential any personal data learned incidentally from official matters; the specified data-protection provisions, namely Bundesdatenschutzgesetz, Sozialgesetzbuch and Hessisches Datenschutzgesetz, together with § 30 AO (Steuergeheimnis), must be observed. (Facts 5, 7) (41bdd431, 0ba37963)
- The tenant may require or carry out a besondere förmliche Verpflichtung of personnel working at or administering the leased property under the Verpflichtungsgesetz, and may require the respective landlord to stop personnel who breach the preceding provisions from working on the administration of this lease or other leases of the Landes Hessen. (Facts 6, 8) (f5fe6d51, 447b2326)
- §18 BESONDERE BEDINGUNGEN provides that the Besonderen Bedingungen Anlagen MV 18 (1) bis (39) additionally apply to the Mietverträge. (Fact 9) (12643ae1)
- If, on resale of the property under § 1.1, the lease does not automatically transfer to the purchaser by law under § 566 Abs. 1 BGB, the respective landlord must transfer it so that the purchaser assumes the landlord’s rights and obligations arising during its ownership, and the tenant gives advance consent; only the law of the Bundesrepublik Deutschland applies to the lease. (Facts 10–11) (7b0b7f41, 0397662d)

## Areas and plans

- The plan covers 2. Untergeschoss (Second Basement). (1f09c937, 8232036e)
- The fire instructions state: 1. Brand melden / 1. Report the fire; activate the alarm with Hausalarm betätigen and call Feuerwehr anrufen (0)112 / Dial (0)112 for the fire brigade, stating Wo brennt es? / Where is the fire?, Was brennt? / What is burning?, Wie viel brennt? / How much is burning?, Welche Gefahren? / What are the dangers?, and then Warten auf Rückfragen! / Wait for further questions! (112ea628, 27acf1cd, c93fd779, 4953be5b, 32b4c53b, d96d3e17, 64ee4b0d, 3641023e)
- The second fire-response action is 2. In Sicherheit bringen / 2. Get people to safety: Aufzug nicht benutzen / Do not use the elevator, Gefährdete Personen mitnehmen / Escort people particularly at risk, Türen schließen / Close all doors, Gekennzeichneten Fluchtwegen folgen / Follow arrows for exit, Fluchtweg zum Sammelpunkt aufsuchen / Follow the instructions leading to assembly point, and Auf Anweisungen achten / Follow the instructions. (dd9719b1, 7b2e48a7, 9ec399a8, 7bae40d6, 9ab07101, 84f48f49, 671c7026, e4452d35)
- The third fire-response action is 3. Löschversuch unternehmen / 3. Attempt to extinguish, using Wenn ohne Gefahr möglich: / Only if possible without risk, Feuerlöscher / Fire extinguisher, Wandhydrant/Löschschlauch / Fire hose, other equipment, and Mittel und Geräte zur Brandbekämpfung. (640db356, 23b39a25, 1db7ad9a, 2d78d3f9, 5813b229)
- The plan repeats the second emergency-evacuation step as 2. In Sicherheit bringen, requiring Aufzug nicht benutzen, Gefährdete Personen mitnehmen, Türen schließen, Gekennzeichneten Fluchtwegen folgen, Sammelstelle aufsuchen and Auf Anweisungen achten. (fdcd260b, 4f314936, 2b321938, 4170e558, 469d125f, 3714810f, 4028cc9d)
- The plan repeats the third step as 3. Löschversuch unternehmen, with Wenn ohne Gefahr möglich: Feuerlöscher, Wandhydrant/ Löschschlauch, Mittel und Geräte zur Brandbekämpfung benutzen; firefighting equipment may be used only if this can be done without danger. (40a59021, 1937b86e)
- For an accident, the first step is 1. Unfall melden / Report the accident; call Rettungsdienst anrufen (0)112 / Dial (0)112 for rescue services and state Was geschehen? / What happened?, Wo geschah es? / Where did it happen?, Wie viele Verletzte? / How many are injured?, and Welche Arten von Verletzungen? / What kind of injuries? - The page headed Verhalten bei Unfällen instructs users to Absicherung des Unfallortes / Secure the accident scene, Versorgung der Verletzten / Provide first aid, Anweisungen beachten / Follow instructions, Weitere Maßnahmen / Further measures, Krankenwagen oder Rettungsdienste einweisen / Direct ambulance or rescue services to the scene of the injury, and Schaulustige entfernen / Instruct curious bystanders to leave; only FRP_Amtsgericht_Am Steinkaut 10-12_final.pdf speaks to these matters, while fluchtplan carries no stated tier and was read as the weakest, tier 6 correspondence (weakest). - The 2024-09-30 survey in 240925 WKP L2-03 GL Brandschutz Wartungsbericht BST 09-24 HKU.pdf records for OG03 4 one-leaf fire doors, 2 two-leaf fire doors and 3 FSA entries; for OG02 2 one-leaf fire doors, 4 two-leaf fire doors and 4 FSA entries; for OG01 10 one-leaf fire doors, 9 two-leaf fire doors and 11 FSA entries; for EG00 14 one-leaf fire doors, 10 two-leaf fire doors and 14 FSA entries; and for UG01 37 one-leaf fire doors, 4 two-leaf fire doors and 5 FSA entries; this is a tier 4 survey (condition). (f0ef5adf, 0b567b47, c5e028c0, 503d5d42, de0acb74)
- The 2025-09-24 survey in 251006 WKP 2.03 GL Wartungsbericht BrandschutztÅren Wartung 09-25 (Kunkel, Heiko).pdf requires PRÜ = Auf Verschmutzung, Beschädigung, Korrosion und Befestigung prüfen, FR = Funktionserhaltendes Reinigen, and SGP = Schließvorrichtung auf Gangbarkeit prüfen; this is a tier 4 survey (condition). (94928fe8, 972826b4, a96b0d16)
- The 251006 WKP 2.03 GL Wartungsbericht BrandschutztÅren Wartung 09-25 (Kunkel, Heiko).pdf, a wartungsbericht ranked as tier 4 survey (condition), dated 2025-09-24 and read on page 4, states: BTS = Bewegliche Teile schmieren (moving parts must be lubricated); SFR = Schließfolgeregler auf Funktion prüfen (the closing-sequence controller must be checked for proper function); PEF = Prüfung aller Elemente der Feststellanlage gegenüber Plan der installierten Elemente (all elements of the hold-open system must be checked against the plan of the installed elements); TH = Tür durch Handauslösung oder Handsteuerung freigeben, wenn anwendbar. Türflügel müssen kontrolliert durch die Türschließmittel schließen (where applicable, the door must be released by manual triggering or manual control, and the door leaves must close in a controlled manner through the door-closing devices); PL = Prüfung auf Leichtgängigkeit der Türflügel. Wenn erforderlich, nachstellen der Türschließmittel (the door leaves must be checked for ease of movement and the closing devices adjusted if necessary); and PB = Prüfen, ob alle Bauteile und Türbeschläge sicher befestigt sind (all components and door fittings must be checked for secure attachment). (f2cdbbff, 852c990c, 1b426bb0, f6497a19, 668c1edb, 5f743459)
- The same 251006 WKP 2.03 GL Wartungsbericht BrandschutztÅren Wartung 09-25 (Kunkel, Heiko).pdf, a wartungsbericht ranked as tier 4 survey (condition), dated 2025-09-24 and read on pages 4–5, states: TÖA = Tür wieder öffnen, so dass sie erneut festgestellt wird. Auslösen der Tür durch Stromunterbrechung. Türflügel müssen kontrolliert durch die Türschließmittel schließen (the door must be reopened so that it is held open again, then triggered by interrupting the power supply, and the door leaves must close in a controlled manner through the door-closing devices); TÖB = Tür wieder öffnen, so dass sie erneut festgestellt wird. Auslösen der Tür (the door must be reopened so that it is held open again and then triggered, with the remainder of the procedure not shown on this unit); durch Simulation des Brandmerkmals der automatischen Melder. Türflügel müssen kontrolliert durch die Türschließmittel schließen (the fire characteristic of the automatic detectors is simulated, and the door leaves must close in a controlled manner using the door-closing devices); PRA (the page labels the following requirement or test item as “PRA”, but its meaning is not stated on this unit); and, for hold-open systems with emergency power batteries only, Prüfung der Ausgangsspannung und des Ausgangsstromes der Batterie nach zweimaliger Unterbrechung der Netzstromversorgung, sofort und nach 30 min. prüfen ob die abgelesenen Werte den festgelegten Werten entsprechen (the battery’s output voltage and output current must be tested after the mains power supply has been interrupted twice, immediately and after 30 minutes, to check whether the recorded values correspond to the specified values). (d0feba16, 93bdb37c, 44c4cdbe, 5cd556ca, 8b1b29bf)
- The defect legend in the 251006 WKP 2.03 GL Wartungsbericht BrandschutztÅren Wartung 09-25 (Kunkel, Heiko).pdf, a wartungsbericht ranked as tier 4 survey (condition), dated 2025-09-24 and read on page 6, defines 1 = Standflügel hängt am Boden oder Rahmen (the inactive door leaf hangs on the floor or frame); 2 = Gehflügel hängt am Boden oder Rahmen (the active door leaf hangs on the floor or frame); 3 = Schließblech nacharbeiten / fehlt / defekt (the strike plate requires reworking, is missing, or is defective); 4 = Zarge nicht fachgerecht eingebaut / defekt / lose (the door frame was not professionally installed, is defective, or is loose); 5 = Türgummi fehlt / defekt / zu kurz (the door seal is missing, defective, or too short); 6 = Obertürschließer /Bodentürschließer/ Federband defekt (the overhead door closer, floor door closer, or spring hinge is defective); and 7 = Absenkbare Bodendichtung fehlt/ defekt/ Spaltmass zu groß (the drop-down floor seal is missing or defective, or the gap is too large). (b578cacb, 7b7528ef, d9d98bdb, 94036df4, 09b000af, 398d6824, 7a5451a6)
- The same defect legend on page 6 defines 9 = Schließfolge defekt (the closing sequence is defective); 10 = Türblatt verzogen (the door leaf is warped); 11 = Aufschaumdichtung fehlt (the foam seal is missing); 12 = Türgriff defekt / fehlt (the door handle is defective or missing); 13 = Panikverriegelung defekt/Panikverriegelung fehlt komplett (the panic locking mechanism is defective or completely missing); 14 = Blindzylinder fehlt, bzw. Schließzylinder (the dummy cylinder or, alternatively, the locking cylinder is missing); 15 = Tür schließt nicht (the door does not close); and 16 = Schlosskasten defekt (the lock case is defective). (dcc122cb, 3c3abdad, c17f298f, 735a7d17, 1f10203a, cf5607f9, 48962afa, 92e918bb)
- The same defect legend on page 6 defines 17 = RM/ Raucherkennungsteil laut Hersteller abgelaufen (the RM smoke detection component has expired according to the manufacturer); 18 = Deckenmelder fehlen (über 1m) (ceiling detectors are missing where the distance is over 1 metre); 19 = FSA ohne Funktion (Magnet hält nicht) Plattenmagnet defekt (the hold-open system is not functioning because the magnet does not hold, and the plate magnet is defective); 20 = FSA löst nicht aus (the hold-open system does not activate); 21 = Obertürschließer Zweiflüglig fehlen - Gleitschiene (the overhead door closers or slide rail for a double-leaf door are missing); 22 = Obertürschließer fehlt Einflüglig (the overhead door closer for a single-leaf door is missing); 23 = Löcher in Tür (there are holes in the door); and 24 = Standflügelverriegelung defekt (the inactive door leaf locking mechanism is defective). (04fb979c, 733d7678, eee62cd4, 156c67a9, a2c75720, 149b12ff, 7b8a0c4c, 24f1ca5b)
- The same defect legend on pages 6–7 defines 25 = Tür war verkeilt (the door was wedged open); 26 = Besondere Mängel an Brandschutztüren (there are special defects affecting fire doors); 27 = Obertürschließer ist ausgehängt (the overhead door closer has been unhooked); 28 = Raucherkennungsteil RM verschmutzt / defekt (the RM smoke detection component is dirty or defective); 29 = Einbau Feststellanlage empfohlen (installation of a hold-open system is recommended); and 30 = Tür war verstellt/festgebunden (the door was misaligned or fixed/tied in place). (71905f3c, b7854a4c, 71910786, 0a8ef327, be37c6d3, 09c10fed)

## The lease

- The landlords and tenants enter into the lease agreements attached to this deed as Annex MV together with additional annexes, and clarify that these are separate independent lease agreements for each property identified under § 1. (Facts 1–2) (4c45ec91, fd87b6f2)
- For each property listed in § 1, or under § 2 together with Annex KV and § 3 together with Annex MV, the parties conclude a separate contractual package consisting of a purchase agreement and a lease agreement; the packages are, in their existence and implementation, subject to a further statement that continues beyond page 7, and remain independent from the packages concluded for the other properties, subject to § 4.2. (Facts 3–5) (948ea322, 473b6211, ba58a52e)
- Under § 4.2, if the seller is entitled under the purchase agreements to rescind one or more purchase agreements and/or claim damages because of the buyer’s breaches, the seller is also entitled, but not obliged, to rescind all other purchase agreements jointly and/or claim damages, including where individual purchase agreements involve no breach; however, the seller may not rescind a purchase agreement or claim damages under it where no breach exists unless the seller simultaneously rescinds all purchase agreements and/or claims damages. (Fact 6) (b7669b4f)
- For each property, the purchase agreement and Mietvertrag form a legal unit: if one is ineffective, not performed or unwound, the other agreement for that property is likewise ineffective, not performed or unwound in its entirety, while ordinary or extraordinary termination of the Mietvertrag by notice does not affect the corresponding purchase agreement. (Fact 7) (0f38ba2a)
- The buyer authorizes Silke Weimar and Britta Habermehl, and the seller authorizes Carmen Arnold and Beate Ochs, each acting individually and all dienstansässig at Goethestraße 31-33, 60313 Frankfurt am Main, to make declarations still required to perform the agreement, apply for deletions, provide identity confirmations and, if necessary, repeat the conveyance declaration; the sentence continues beyond page 8, and the authorised persons may make and receive declarations and notices under § 1280 BGB. (Facts 8–9) (0bd77130, 2d452f3c)
- The power of attorney takes effect immediately, regardless of whether any official approvals required for the contract’s validity have been obtained. (Fact 10) (ce3b5697)
- The power of attorney permits the authorised persons to create Grundpfandrechte up to the purchase price, including a Gesamtgrundschuld up to the total purchase price, with up to 15 % annual interest and up to 10 % one-time ancillary benefits; to make Schuldanerkenntnisse for the buyer; to submit the buyer to immediate Zwangsvollstreckung in his entire assets and the respective owner of the purchase property to immediate Zwangsvollstreckung in the encumbered real property; to approve and apply for the corresponding registrations; and, when creating a Grundschuld, to extend to 30 years the limitation period for claims for return of the securities, including claims arising from the assumption of personal liability. (Fact 11) (83ddec4a)
- The persons named in § 5, Ziffer 5.1 may use the foregoing encumbrance powers of attorney only if the buyer confirms to the notary in writing, including by fax or e-mail, that the buyer and any possible trusted persons are unable to act as representatives. (Fact 12) (c9c5188f)
- The notary advised that Grundpfandrechtsgläubiger generally require the buyer to give a notarial Schuldanerkenntnis and submit all of the buyer’s assets to immediate Zwangsvollstreckung under the notarial deed for the acknowledged claim, and informed the buyer of the resulting consequences; the buyer instructed the authorised persons to make such Schuldanerkenntnisse and the specified submissions to enforcement on his behalf, and to make all other declarations required in connection with creating the Grundschuld insofar as required by the forms supplied to them. (Facts 13–14) (c50588d1, 338955d3)
- The power of attorney to create Grundpfandrechte under § 4 of the purchase agreements applies only if the ordering deed contains the provisions in § 4.1 (a) to (f) of the purchase agreements. (Fact 15) (61d33f56)
- The restrictions on the power of attorney apply only in the internal relationship; externally, the power of attorney is unrestricted. (Fact 16) (ca67fcf8)
- The power of attorney may be used only for a notarial record or certification of signatures by the acting notary. (Fact 17) (6711d144)
- The powers of attorney expire one year after registration of the transfer of ownership; the sentence continues beyond the page and states that they are jederzeit, with the remainder not shown on this unit. (Fact 18) (f4c3fa67)
- The authorised representatives have no personal liability unless they act with gross negligence or intent, but this limitation does not apply in the event of injury to life, body and/or health. (Fact 19) (8250ade2)
- The parties also authorize the notary, where necessary, to amend and supplement approvals and applications before the Grundbuchamt, take all procedurally necessary steps to implement the agreement, correct or supplement Grundstücksbezeichnungen and/or designate them under § 28 GBO, and apply to divide properties into legally independent properties. (Fact 20) (84a1ae37)
- Each party bears its own representation costs, including approvals, confirmations of powers of attorney and advisers’ costs; the seller bears the costs of deleting unassumed encumbrances, registering the beschränkten persönlichen Dienstbarkeiten under § 14 of the Mietverträge and the Dienstbarkeiten/Baulasten provided for in the Besonderen Bedingungen to the purchase agreements, and dividing properties where parts are sold; the buyer bears all other costs of this deed and the agreements concluded with it and their implementation, as well as Grunderwerbsteuer. (Fact 21) (57c12e2c)
- The notary advised that the parties are legally jointly and severally liable for Grunderwerbsteuer and costs regardless of the contractual allocation. (Fact 22) (62b16ef7)
- The parties confirm the powers of attorney of the persons acting for them as authorised representatives in the Bezugsurkunde. (Fact 23) (853c1a07)
- The power of attorney is dated 01.11.2006. (Fact 24) (f5a6e95f)
- The issuing person states, “Hiermit bevollmächtige ich”, to act in the name of the State of Hesse concerning the real property listed below. (Facts 25–26) (813abaf9, 55e00ac8)
- The issuer authorizes Ministerialrat Hans-Joachim Hertling, born on 25. Februar 1950 and dienstansässig at Friedrich-Ebert-Allee 8, 65185 Wiesbaden, to act in the name of Landes Hessen in relation to the real property listed below. (Fact 27) (3efe7288)
- The power of attorney includes declaring and receiving the Auflassung, as stated both in the general authority and in the property-specific authority. (Facts 28–29) (d30e0a32, 27ff035e)
- The power of attorney includes arranging for Vermessungen and making Identitätserklärungen, as stated both in the general authority and in the property-specific authority. (Facts 30–31) (68e41e5b, 021ab163)
- The power of attorney includes creating Belastungen of the real property in Abt. II und III, including submitting the respective Grundstückseigentümer to immediate Zwangsvollstreckung in the real property, as stated both in the general authority and in the property-specific authority. - Changes and additions to the Mietvertrag, including its Nachträge, are effective only if agreed between the parties in a Nachtrag zum Mietvertrag satisfying the Schriftformerfordernisse of §§ 578, 550 Satz 1, 126 BGB; the same applies to agreements intended to waive that besondere Schriftformerfordernis generally or in an individual case, but not where the change concerns a provision subject to the Recht der Allgemeinen Geschäftsbedingungen (§§ 305 ff. BGB), in which case the parties’ agreement applies regardless of form. The parties adopted these rules in light of the Bundesgerichtshofs decisions of 27. September 2017, Aktenzeichen XII ZR 114/16, on sog. Schriftformheilungsklauseln and of 25. Januar 2017, Aktenzeichen XII ZR 69/16, on sog. doppelter Schriftformklauseln. NT zum MV.docx, part 2, 2025-05-23.] (fd94afd6, 2fffc437, abff290b, 7ee3b211)

- The tenant must obtain any required official approvals at its own expense. (Facts 1, 2) (f79b80f4, e4674d9d)
- For a change of use, the tenant must, at its own cost and holding the landlord harmless, satisfy all resulting requirements concerning the condition of the leased property; repair and maintenance remain governed by § 6. (Fact 3) (09c2bc1b)
- The tenant may use the entire leased property, including open areas, building façades and roofs, for its own advertising, or make it available to third parties, for payment or free of charge, for advertising or other purposes such as operating a mobile communications/UMTS antenna installation or a photovoltaic installation; this includes the right to install or erect advertising installations, including illuminated advertising, photovoltaic installations or other installations and to make necessary structural alterations. - Each tenancy begins on the date possession is transferred, the handover date, under § 9 of the purchase agreements, and has a term of 30 years. (Facts 8, 9) (85ce2f80, 863a5099)
- After the 30-year term expires, the tenancy renews for successive three-year periods unless it is terminated no later than 12 months before the end of the lease term; it does not become indefinite merely because the tenant continues using the premises and neither party objects, and § 545 BGB does not apply. (Facts 10, 11) (7debc4e9, 88448fed)
- The tenant is entitled to terminate each tenancy, potentially only for the specified part (Teilkündigung), at the stated times subject to a notice period of 12 months; one stated time is 25 years after the start of the lease. (Facts 12–14) (db29b0ab, 303f3414, bcda928b)
- The termination provisions state no early termination for Marburger Straße 2-4, Ostpa... 7, 15, 17, 19; 25 Jahre nach Mietbeginn for Hofheim, Zeilsheimer Straße 59; and 25 Jahre nach Mietbeginn for Idstein, Gerichtsstraße 1 und 3. (Facts 15–17) (ddb1ef3a, 03dae87e, 38412439)
- For Marburg, Robert-Koch-Straße 5-17, termination is possible for Building 9 at 2, 5, 10, 15, 20, and 25 years after the start of the lease, and for the remaining buildings and all parking spaces at 25 years after the start of the lease. (Facts 18–20) (31e982ff, 43d164e6, 407f1267)
- For Marburg, Universitätsstraße 48-50, no early termination is permitted; for Rüsselsheim, Eisenstraße 60 and Rüsselsheim, Johann-Sebastian-Bach-Straße 45, the stated partial-termination date is 25 years after the lease commencement. (Facts 21–23) (0c2236ee, 4aa709ee, 004da5a1)
- The stated partial-termination date is 25 years after the lease commencement for Wiesbaden, Schaperstraße 16, Wiesbaden, Willy-Brandt-Allee 2 and Wiesbaden, Willy-Brandt-Allee 20-22. (Facts 24–26) (999fff56, bff66a3d, ea8e6efc)

- All deadlines are determined by when the relevant declaration reaches its recipient, and termination of the tenancy for good cause is governed by statutory provisions. (Facts 28, 29) (839c2093, 8d56e310)
- Defects existing at the start of the lease are handled under § 6; each respective landlord must remedy the defects or perform the works described in Anlage MV 3.2 (1) bis (39) zur Bezugsurkunde, regardless of their allocation under §§ 6.1 or 6.2, starting without delay after lease commencement and completing within a reasonable period. The tenant has reduction, damages or termination rights for qualifying defects and works only if the relevant landlord is in default with the remedy. (Facts 30, 31) (9a4b4d0d, 57b3b49e)
- The tenant is responsible for the permits existing at lease commencement for the premises’ current use, including associated conditions and ancillary provisions, and for other administrative matters existing at lease commencement; compliance with relevant official orders is at the tenant’s own cost unless § 3.2 provides otherwise, while the respective landlord ensures that the premises remain usable for their current use and is liable for complying with official orders issued after the lease begins. (Facts 32, 33) (cf72047e, 4ad4b2a9)
- The tenant has no right to reduce the rent or claim damages from the respective landlord if the tenant, including through persons it appoints, conducts investigations or excavations on the property to identify or recover archaeological monuments under § 19 Hessisches Denkmalschutzgesetz. (Fact 34) (e03a20ec)
- Clause 4.1 provides that the annual rent for each respective leased property is composed as described in Anlagen MV 1.2 (1) bis (39) zur Bezugsurkunde. (Facts 35, 36) (24e1ef45, 1eff01b9)
- The tenant must pay one twelfth of the rent monthly in advance, by the third working day of each month, to an account designated by the landlord; payment is timely only when the money has been received in the landlord’s account. (Facts 37, 38) (b3a03b5a, 2ec20bd6)
- The tenant is entitled and obliged, insofar as legally possible, to operate and manage the leased property itself at its own cost, except for the landlord’s obligation under § 6, at the landlord’s own cost, to maintain and repair the roof and structural fabric, including servicing, inspection and necessary replacement or renewal. - Under section 5.2, in addition to rent, the tenant bears all operating and ancillary costs under the currently applicable Betriebskostenverordnung attached to the reference deed as Anlage MV 5.2 (a), the costs of the repair and maintenance of the leased property for which it is responsible under section 6, including servicing, inspection and necessary replacement or renewal, and insurance costs only to the extent provided for under section 13. - Under section 5.3, the tenant pays the operating and ancillary costs directly where possible; costs incurred by the respective landlord are invoiced to the tenant when incurred, and the respective landlord’s invoices are due one month after receipt by the tenant. (2d0b3bc4)
- Under section 5.4, if the tenant has not rented all the land and buildings specified in section 1.1, it bears the operating and ancillary costs proportionately; the parties are to agree the allocation key and payment method, or, if they cannot agree, a jointly appointed Sachverständiger is to determine them for both parties under § 317 BGB. (80a937dd)

(2c954eb3, fd6c79b3)
- An email dated 2023-01-26 revisits contractual responsibility for the drainage channel in the underground car park and rejects the argument that § 6.1 of the Leo II lease makes the landlord responsible for maintaining or repairing it; it states that the lease definition of Dach applies only to roof gutters and not to underground-car-park channels, and that the cited wording on coverings and plumbing works—including 'Eindeckung und die dazu gehörigen Klempnerarbeiten (Dachrinnen, Fallrohre, Verwahrung, Bodeneinläufe wie z. B. Gullys, etc.)'—concerns only the roof or roof structures, so the channels are outside the definition Dach i.S.d. Mietvertrages Leo II (facts reported only in AW_ L2-03, Bad Homburg, 21-2.03-32212 FeuchtigkeitsschÑden KG.pdf, tier 6 correspondence (weakest)). (fb07813d, 5ceb5015, ef40d207, efafb6b2, 8a65aa0f)
- The same email states that the underground-car-park channels are also outside the definition Fach of the Mietvertrages Leo II because they are not expressly mentioned, and that they are separate components rather than constructive parts of the Gebäude Tiefgarage or of its structural elements; on that basis, it assigns responsibility for their repair to the tenant. (29f86a20, de18ed18, 7233d9ab)
- The same email states that, under the lease, Bodeneinläufe and Bodenrinnen are not the landlord’s responsibility and that cleaning, maintaining water-drainage capability and further repairs to Bodenrinnen and Abläufen in the Tiefgarage are the tenant’s responsibility. (e8b8fcea, f0ae0342)
- The same email also records an overview stating that the landlord is responsible for Bodenabläufe such as gullies, but says that the sender could not establish from the available documents who is responsible for cleaning exterior gullies and the floor drains in the underground garage, and asks whether Firma Spie is responsible or whether a lease provision governs the issue. (09b493f4, 38d67753, 2374140a)
- The email disputes the recipient’s interpretation of the lease and relies on §6, headed §6 INSTANDHALTUNG UND INSTANDSETZUNG DER MIETSACHE / SCHÖNHEITSREPARATUREN; § 6.1 states that the respective landlord must, at its own cost, maintain and repair the leased property, including Wartung, Inspektion and necessary replacement or renewal, at Dach und Fach. (f9468589, afee67c8, 28af043d)
- The lease definition 'Dach' covers the roof structures of all buildings, the uppermost levels of all parking decks, and all ceilings and intermediate ceilings of the underground garages, including insulation and waterproofing against drückendes and nicht drückendes Wasser, as well as coverings and related plumbing works including Dachrinnen, Fallrohre, Verwahrung and Bodeneinläufe such as Gullys, together with Vor-, Neben- und Glasdächer and the roof entrances and exits. (0a512682)
- The sender therefore requires the recipient to comply with the lease, arrange for the gullies to be cleaned and ensure that these areas are included in FMC’s monthly safety inspections. (8e28d638)
- The sender definitively rejects the recipient’s position as neither understandable nor supportable under the lease, while proposing a higher-level discussion of the Mietvertragsauslegung if the recipient does not accept the position set out in the email dated 05.09.2022. (8d839761, 0d2604d3, 2d96d284)
- The sender says that no reply had been made because the recipient had announced in May that a meaningful FAMOS evaluation would be sent for coordination or discussion, and states that an attached email on a similar topic had been sent to the recipient and Frau Pohl in September but had received no response up to the date of the message. (a7b1544d, 536f54e0, 39ad0ac9)
- The sender thanks Ms Kahle and says that he will arrange an appointment for higher-level coordination on the following Wednesday; if further discussion is considered necessary, he offers a telephone call tomorrow from 10.30 Uhr, next Wednesday except during 11 - 13 Uhr, or Thursday. (3474515a, 083e9ca3)

## Fire safety

- The fire protection meets the requirements of fire-resistance class F 90, as stated only in StatikprÅfberichte_Erweiterungsneubau, 1988-1990.pdf, read on 1990-02-23. (1302881f)
- In an accident, the first step is to report it by calling Rettungsdienst at (0)112, stating where and what happened, how many people are injured and the types of injuries, and waiting for further questions. (a568b83b, 10200187, 88d344b4, ae69ba25, e08b52e2, 4b1961b1)
- In the event of fire, people are instructed to remain calm and, as the first step, report the fire by activating Hausalarm betätigen and calling Feuerwehr at (0)112; they should state where and what is burning, how much is burning and what hazards exist, then wait for further questions. (8a3ef09d, ebc452b5, b97a80ed, f7de5c41, 6353f2da, a754d9ce, b8fcd0e6, eae3c8c9, d119a66d, 9aae0079, 739cbc93)
- The second fire-response step is to bring people to safety: do not use the elevator, take or escort people at risk, close doors, follow the marked escape routes or exit arrows to the Fluchttreppen, Fluchtweg or Sammelstelle, and follow instructions. (a77446fe, dddb86ea, a2b532b4, f2cc1e0b, 3d481c6c, 45d35303, 64697d04, b07a8ecf, 61712220, bc3b9076, f9badb07, 6104220e, 0351853b, f3dc561e, 2d0dc558)
- The third fire-response step is to attempt firefighting, but only wenn ohne Gefahr möglich, using Feuerlöscher, Wandhydrant/Löschschlauch or Wandhydrant/ Löschschlauch and other Mittel und Geräte zur Brandbekämpfung. (716d4cd0, f3fbfd7d, b4b8a07d, cab86294, 3d98c64e, 09ab9164, 381b699f)
- The fire-response instructions are headed “Verhalten im Brandfall / In case of fire” and state that the first step is “1. Brand melden”, the second is “2. In Sicherheit bringen”, and the third is “3. Löschversuch unternehmen”; the instructions also say “Verhalten im Brandfall Ruhe bewahren In case of fire keep calm”. These points are stated only in FRP_Amtsgericht_Am Steinkaut 10-12_final.pdf, read on 2025-10-01 at pages 8, 9, 10, 11 and 12. (6b3278ac, c0fafde5, 743d542c, 985cceab, adebff44, ac0e3d2c, 13c49321, c9362654, 19f38c12, e9319ea7)
- When reporting a fire, the instructions say “Hausalarm betätigen / Activate alarm button” and “Feuerwehr anrufen (0)112 / Dial (0)112 for the fire brigade”; the caller must answer “Wo brennt? / Where is the fire?”, “Was brennt? / What is burning?”, “Wie viel brennt? / How much is burning?”, “Welche Gefahren? / What are the dangers?” and “Weitere Fragen abwarten! / Wait for further questions!”. These points are stated only in FRP_Amtsgericht_Am Steinkaut 10-12_final.pdf, read on 2025-10-01 at page 11. (960dbd7c, f3ab7051, c80be7a8, b9ff4da4, 85f066c8, 615656e2, b2fab9c0)
- For evacuation, the instructions are “Aufzug nicht benutzen / Gefährdete Personen mitnehmen / Türen schließen / Gekennzeichneten Fluchtwegen folgen / Sammelstelle aufsuchen / Auf Anweisungen achten”: do not use the elevator, escort people particularly at risk, close doors, follow the marked escape routes and exit arrows, follow the indicated route to the assembly point, and follow instructions. The same measures are stated under “2. In Sicherheit bringen Aufzug nicht benutzen Gefährdete Personen mitnehmen Türen schließen Gekennzeichneten Fluchtwegen folgen Auf Anweisungen achten”. These points are stated only in FRP_Amtsgericht_Am Steinkaut 10-12_final.pdf, read on 2025-10-01 at pages 10, 11 and 12. (d3963ce9, 7bd14607, 92770d3e, 437f86a4, 3f317b8a, 74762811, b2b5fd99, f1403d6b)
- A fire may be fought only if this is possible without danger: “Wenn ohne Gefahr möglich: Feuerlöscher / Wandhydrant/Löschschlauch / Mittel und Geräte zur Brandbekämpfung benutzen”; the listed equipment is a “Feuerlöscher / Fire extinguisher”, “Wandhydrant/ Löschschlauch / Fire hose,” and “Mittel und Geräte zur Brandbekämpfung benutzen / Fire hose, other equipment for firefighting”. The third step is “3. Löschversuch unternehmen Wenn ohne Gefahr möglich: Feuerlöscher Wandhydrant/Löschschlauch Mittel und Geräte zur Brandbekämpfung benutzen”. These points are stated only in FRP_Amtsgericht_Am Steinkaut 10-12_final.pdf, read on 2025-10-01 at pages 8, 9, 10, 11 and 12. (e39c8ed3, fc702bae, eee116c9, f5b93410, 7d89c529, 248a4af7, cfee970f)
- The accident instructions are headed “Verhalten bei Unfällen / In case of accidents” and state that the first step is “1. Unfall melden”, the second is “2. Erste Hilfe”, and the third is “3. Weitere Maßnahmen”; the corresponding accident-response steps are “1. Unfall melden / 1. Report the accident”, “2. Erste Hilfe / 2. First aid”, and “3. Weitere Maßnahmen / 3. Further measures”. These points are stated only in FRP_Amtsgericht_Am Steinkaut 10-12_final.pdf, read on 2025-10-01 at pages 10 and 11. (2dae5c6f, c2454e02, 4c827764, 59f0040e, ba678baa, af7fc731, 26a2bb97)
- The accident instructions also say “Verhalten bei Unfällen Ruhe bewahren In case of accidents keep calm”. This point is stated only in FRP_Amtsgericht_Am Steinkaut 10-12_final.pdf, read on 2025-10-01 at page 12. (7552f1cd)
- In case of an accident, call Rettungsdienst at (0)112, state “Wo geschah es?”, “Was geschah?”, the number of injured persons and “Welche Arten von Verletzungen?”, and wait for “Rückfragen!”; the document also states “Rettungsdienst anrufen (0)112”, “Wo geschah es?”, “Was geschah?”, “Welche Arten von Verletzungen?” and “Warten auf Rückfragen!”. (53e62b3b, 3d3b598a, eefe78a9, 1ac73659, 8fc55f34, f2b01af0, 599318d7)
- Provide first aid by “Absicherung des Unfallortes”, “Versorgung der Verletzten” and “Anweisungen beachten”; the accident scene must be secured, injured persons cared for and instructions followed. (7a25c136, e24bdc6d, 54835b12, d15c3d56, d3a01009)
- Minor repairs that can be performed during inspection and maintenance, including adjustment and lubrication, are carried out immediately: “Kleinreparaturen, die bei Prüfung und Wartung möglich sind (einstellen, fetten usw.) werden sofort mit ausgeführt.” (24e3eb45)
- Under § 6.2 of the Rahmenmietvertrag, the tenant is responsible for the Instandhaltung/Instandsetzung of the Brandmeldeanlage, including Wartung, Inspektion, required replacement or renewal, the corresponding Aufschaltungen, and the preparation and updating of required technical documentation, particularly the Brandmeldepläne. (73dffa05)
- The tenant is responsible for all updates and revisions of the Fluchtwegepläne currently located at the leased premises, especially those required by the tenant’s construction measures, and must determine whether such updates or revisions are or have become necessary and carry them out, or have them carried out, immediately at its own expense. (93659f00, 0528ec52)
- The same responsibility applies to the Feuerwehrpläne of the leased premises. (a5583edc)
- The parties use, with service providers involved, an electronic tool called the “FAMOS-Liste” to record damage and report defects, construction measures, maintenance and repair measures and similar matters connected with the lease; the parties use it in particular to record and comment on matters requiring action and assign them a processing status, but there is no future obligation to use it. (571b66d4, 4c2a0c00)
- Agreements between the parties concerning the operation of the “FAMOS-Liste”, including recording, commenting and processing statuses, are not legally binding until documented in any required schriftformgerechten form and are only non-binding preliminary understandings. (e21f0595)

## Energy performance

- The document is an ENERGIEAUSWEIS für Nichtwohngebäude, issued on 09.04.2021 Ausstellungsdatum and valid until Gültig bis: 08.04.2031; it is available in the data room and is also stated there to be gültig bis 08.04.2031. (7f95f988, fe00a7ce, c24d6a75, 307bb345)
- The certificate is a consumption-based certificate: ☑ Der Energieausweis wurde auf der Grundlage von Auswertungen des Energieverbrauchs erstellt (Energieverbrauchsausweis), with results on page 3 and comparison values based on statistical evaluations; ☐ Der Energieausweis wurde auf der Grundlage von Berechnungen des Energiebedarfs erstellt (Energiebedarfsausweis) is not marked, and the owner collected the demand/consumption data: Datenerhebung Bedarf/Verbrauch durch ☑ Eigentümer. (a118a260, 500860ac, f713e3b5)
- The certificate is for information only, concerns the entire building or the identified building part, and is intended only to enable an approximate comparison of buildings; it identifies the certified building part as Gebäudeteil Liegenschaft. (8c3ebd7b, 66455662)
- The heating generator year shown is Baujahr Wärmeerzeuger 1990. (7dfddc28)
- The page identifies the calculation method used for the energy-demand calculation as ☑ Verfahren nach Anlage 2 Nummer 2 EnEV; the stated demand values are specific EnEV values per square metre of heated or cooled net floor area, and the EnEV permits alternative simplifications for calculating energy demand. (8d973bac, 6e38c30e, e58ef4a9, 46dbfcc9)
- The method for determining energy-consumption indicators is prescribed by the Energieeinsparverordnung; the values are specific values per square metre of heated/cooled net floor area, and actual building consumption may differ because of weather influence and changing user behaviour. (12e4e081)
- The certificate states that cost-effective energy-efficiency improvement measures are possible and recommends 1 Sonstiges Energieberatung für NWG durchführen als Einzelmaßnahme; the modernization recommendations are for information only, are brief indications, and are no substitute for an energy consultation. (57ab41f4, 46a595c5, 07aa16ba, a5254c75)
- For a non-residential building substantially used for residential purposes, the certificate is limited to the building part separately treated as non-residential under § 22 EnEV, identified as "Gebäudeteil". (8fad33a8)
- Under the EEWärmeG, new buildings must use renewable energy to a specified extent to meet heating and cooling demand; the field "Angaben zum EEWärmeG" shows the type of renewable energy used and the percentage of statutory-obligation fulfilment, while the field "Ersatzmaßnahmen" is completed where the EEWärmeG requirements are met partly or fully through energy-saving measures; these details evidence to the competent authority the extent of fulfilment through the substitute measure and compliance with the stricter EnEV requirements applicable to the building. (925b6cf3, 65995e67, e21b6726, 3ab610c2)
- The recorded consumption period runs from 01.01.2019 to 31.12.2019. (f1a8d8ec, 3f785535)

## Environmental contamination

- The Gutachten des Hessischen Landesamtes für Bodenforschung was issued in WIESBADEN on 8. August 1963 for Staatsbauamt Bad Homburg v.d.H.; by letter dated 27. Juni 1962 under file reference Az.: o 6228-43/124-1223/62-9, the client requested Baugrundbeurteilung und Gründungsberatung. [The report is dated 1963-08-08.] (487b99c2, 8fda2847, 2d9d5725, 59ef3aaf)
- The drilled soils are predominantly stiff in consistency, with some layers also being plastic. [This finding is from the 1963 report.] (dcf43823)
- Das Gutachten darf ohne schriftliche Genehmigung des Hess. Landesamtes für Bodenforschung weder ganz noch auszugsweise veröffentlicht werden. (be3defd4)
- The official contaminated-sites information is an Amtliche Auskunft dated 02.06.2025 and issued by Regierungspräsidium Darmstadt. (1c8059d2, 2886634e, cc9a6e49)
- The official information concerning unexploded ordnance is dated 25.04.2006. (fa05b20f)
